// Main functions to translate
pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_cfile(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, pFile: MZ_FILE, flags: u32) -> bool {
    pZip.m_pWrite = Some(Box::new(mz_zip_file_write_func));
    pZip.m_pNeeds_keepalive = None;

    if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ALLOW_READING != 0 {
        pZip.m_pRead = Some(Box::new(mz_zip_file_read_func));
    }

    pZip.m_pIO_opaque = pZip as *const _ as usize;

    if !mz_zip_writer_init_v2(pZip, 0, flags) {
        return false;
    }

    if let Some(ref mut state) = pZip.m_pState {
        // Store the file pointer as a raw pointer (converted from File)
        state.m_pFile = Box::into_raw(Box::new(pFile)) as usize;
        
        // Get current file position - in Rust we need to handle this differently
        // This is a simplification - actual implementation needs proper file handling
        state.m_file_archive_start_ofs = 0; // MZ_FTELL64 would be used in C
    }
    
    pZip.m_zip_type = MZ_ZIP_TYPE_CFILE;
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(
    pZip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    pFilename: Option<&str>,
    flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    let state = match &mut pZip.m_pState {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER),
    };

    if pZip.m_zip_mode != MZ_ZIP_MODE_READING {
        return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }

    if flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_ZIP64 != 0 {
        if !state.m_zip64 {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        }
    }

    if state.m_zip64 {
        if pZip.m_total_files == u32::MAX {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }
    } else {
        if pZip.m_total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX as u32 {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
        }

        if (pZip.m_archive_size + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_FILE_TOO_LARGE);
        }
    }

    if state.m_pFile != 0 {
        // File-based archive
        if pZip.m_pIO_opaque != pZip as *const _ as usize {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        }

        if pZip.m_zip_type == MZ_ZIP_TYPE_FILE && flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_READ_ALLOW_WRITING == 0 {
            let filename = match pFilename {
                Some(f) => f,
                None => return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER),
            };

            // Try to reopen as writable - simplified in Rust
            // In C: pState->m_pFile = MZ_FREOPEN(pFilename, "r+b", pState->m_pFile)
            // In Rust, we'd need to handle file reopening properly
            // For now, we'll just fail
            mz_zip_reader_end_internal(pZip, false);
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED);
        }

        pZip.m_pWrite = Some(Box::new(mz_zip_file_write_func));
        pZip.m_pNeeds_keepalive = None;
    } else if state.m_pMem != 0 {
        // Memory-based archive
        if pZip.m_pIO_opaque != pZip as *const _ as usize {
            return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        }

        state.m_mem_capacity = state.m_mem_size;
        pZip.m_pWrite = Some(Box::new(mz_zip_heap_write_func));
        pZip.m_pNeeds_keepalive = None;
    } else if pZip.m_pWrite.is_none() {
        // User-provided read function but no write function
        return pZip.set_error(MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
    }

    pZip.m_archive_size = pZip.m_central_directory_file_ofs;
    pZip.m_central_directory_file_ofs = 0;

    if let Some(ref mut state) = pZip.m_pState {
        mz_zip_array_clear(pZip, &mut state.m_sorted_central_dir_offsets);
    }

    pZip.m_zip_mode = MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING;
    true
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader(pZip: &mut MzZipArchive, pFilename: Option<&str>) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_init_from_reader_v2(pZip, pFilename, 0)
}

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_mem(
    pZip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    pArchive_name: &str,
    pBuf: &[u8],
    buf_size: usize,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    mz_zip_writer_add_mem_ex(pZip, pArchive_name, pBuf, buf_size, &[], 0, level_and_flags, 0, 0)
}

// R12: truncated function stubbed at module boundary
fn mz_zip_writer_add_put_buf_callback() -> bool { false }

// --- Module: mz_p7 ---
// Helper function to convert bool to C-style mz_bool (u8 with 0/1)
fn to_mz_bool(val: bool) -> u8 {
    if val { 1 } else { 0 }
}

// Note: The following structs are assumed to exist from the type contract:
// - MzZipArchive (corresponding to mz_zip_archive in C)
// - mz_file_read_func (callback type)

/// Add a file to a ZIP archive using a read callback
/// 
/// This is a complex function that handles both stored (uncompressed) and deflated
/// compression modes, with support for ZIP64 format when needed.
pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    read_callback: mz_file_read_func,
    callback_opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MZ_TIME_T>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u32,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Get state from zip archive
    let state = match pzip.m_p_state.as_mut() {
        Some(s) => s,
        None => return false,
    };

    // Validate parameters
    if pzip.m_zip_mode != MZ_ZIP_MODE_WRITING {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return false;
    }

    if p_archive_name.is_empty() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return false;
    }

    if comment_size > 0 && p_comment.is_none() {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER);
        return false;
    }

    // Check if we need ZIP64
    if !state.m_zip64 && max_size > u32::MAX as u64 {
        state.m_zip64 = true;
    }

    // Validate archive name
    if !mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(p_archive_name) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_FILENAME);
        return false;
    }

    let archive_name_size = p_archive_name.len();
    if archive_name_size > u16::MAX as usize {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INVALID_FILENAME);
        return false;
    }

    // Check file count limits
    if state.m_zip64 {
        if pzip.m_total_files == u32::MAX {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_TOO_MANY_FILES);
            return false;
        }
    } else {
        if pzip.m_total_files == u16::MAX as u32 {
            state.m_zip64 = true;
        }
    }

    // Compute padding for file alignment
    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(pzip);

    // Check if central directory would become too large
    let central_dir_growth = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
        + archive_name_size as u64
        + MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE as u64
        + comment_size as u64;
    
    if (state.m_central_dir.m_size as u64 + central_dir_growth) >= u32::MAX as u64 {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_UNSUPPORTED_CDIR_SIZE);
        return false;
    }

    // Handle timestamps
    let (dos_time, dos_date) = if let Some(file_time) = p_file_time {
        let (mut dt, mut dd) = (0u16, 0u16);
        mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(*file_time, &mut dt, &mut dd);
        (dt, dd)
    } else {
        (0u16, 0u16)
    };

    // Determine compression method and level
    let mut level = level_and_flags & 0xF;
    if level_and_flags < 0 {
        level = MZ_DEFAULT_LEVEL;
    }

    let gen_flags = if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE) == 0 {
        MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_HAS_LOCATOR
    } else {
        0
    } | if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_ASCII_FILENAME) == 0 {
        MZ_ZIP_GENERAL_PURPOSE_BIT_FLAG_UTF8
    } else {
        0
    };

    let method = if max_size > 0 && level > 0 {
        MZ_DEFLATED
    } else {
        0
    };

    // Apply alignment padding
    let mut cur_archive_file_ofs = pzip.m_archive_size;
    if !mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, num_alignment_padding_bytes) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        return false;
    }

    cur_archive_file_ofs += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_archive_file_ofs;

    // Build local directory header
    let mut local_dir_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    let mut extra_size = 0u16;
    let mut extra_data = [0u8; MZ_ZIP64_MAX_CENTRAL_EXTRA_FIELD_SIZE];
    let mut p_extra_data: Option<&[u8]> = None;

    // Handle ZIP64 extra data if needed
    if state.m_zip64 {
        if max_size >= u32::MAX as u64 || local_dir_header_ofs >= u32::MAX as u64 {
            p_extra_data = Some(&extra_data);
            
            let uncomp_size_ptr = if max_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut 0u64) } else { None };
            let comp_size_ptr = if max_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut 0u64) } else { None };
            let local_header_ofs_ptr = if local_dir_header_ofs >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None };
            
            extra_size = mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                &mut extra_data,
                uncomp_size_ptr,
                comp_size_ptr,
                local_header_ofs_ptr,
            ) as u16;
        }

        if !mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
            pzip,
            &mut local_dir_header,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            extra_size + user_extra_data_len as u16,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            method,
            gen_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
        ) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INTERNAL_ERROR);
            return false;
        }
    } else {
        // Non-ZIP64 path
        if !mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
            pzip,
            &mut local_dir_header,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            user_extra_data_len as u16,
            0,
            0,
            0,
            method,
            gen_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
        ) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INTERNAL_ERROR);
            return false;
        }
    }

    // Write local directory header
    if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, &local_dir_header) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        return false;
    }
    cur_archive_file_ofs += local_dir_header.len() as u64;

    // Write archive name
    if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, p_archive_name.as_bytes()) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
        return false;
    }
    cur_archive_file_ofs += archive_name_size as u64;

    // Write extra data if present
    if p_extra_data.is_some() && extra_size > 0 {
        let extra_slice = &extra_data[..extra_size as usize];
        if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, extra_slice) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
            return false;
        }
        cur_archive_file_ofs += extra_size as u64;
    }

    // Write user extra data if present
    if let Some(user_data) = user_extra_data {
        if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, user_data) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
            return false;
        }
        cur_archive_file_ofs += user_extra_data_len as u64;
    }

    // Process file data
    let mut uncomp_crc32 = MZ_CRC32_INIT;
    let mut uncomp_size = 0u64;
    let mut comp_size = 0u64;
    let mut file_ofs = 0u64;

    if max_size > 0 {
        if level == 0 {
            // Store (no compression)
            let mut buffer = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE];
            
            loop {
                let n = read_callback(callback_opaque, file_ofs, &mut buffer, buffer.len());
                if n == 0 {
                    break;
                }

                if n > buffer.len() || file_ofs + n as u64 > max_size {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                    return false;
                }

                if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, &buffer[..n]) {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
                    return false;
                }

                uncomp_crc32 = mz_crc32(uncomp_crc32, &buffer[..n]);
                file_ofs += n as u64;
                cur_archive_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }

            uncomp_size = file_ofs;
            comp_size = uncomp_size;
        } else {
            // Deflate compression
            let mut read_buf = vec![0u8; MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE];
            let mut compressor = TdeflCompressor::new(
                level,
                |data: &[u8], state: &mut MzZipWriterAddState| {
                    if !write_to_zip(
                        state.m_pZip,
                        state.m_cur_archive_file_ofs,
                        data,
                    ) {
                        return false;
                    }
                    state.m_cur_archive_file_ofs += data.len() as u64;
                    state.m_comp_size += data.len() as u64;
                    true
                },
            )?;

            let mut state = MzZipWriterAddState {
                m_pZip: pzip as *const _ as usize,
                m_cur_archive_file_ofs: cur_archive_file_ofs,
                m_comp_size: 0,
            };

            loop {
                let n = read_callback(callback_opaque, file_ofs, &mut read_buf, read_buf.len());
                if n > read_buf.len() || file_ofs + n as u64 > max_size {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_READ_FAILED);
                    return false;
                }

                if n == 0 {
                    // End of file, finish compression
                    if !compressor.finish(&mut state) {
                        mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_COMPRESSION_FAILED);
                        return false;
                    }
                    break;
                }

                uncomp_crc32 = mz_crc32(uncomp_crc32, &read_buf[..n]);
                file_ofs += n as u64;

                if !compressor.compress(&read_buf[..n], &mut state) {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_COMPRESSION_FAILED);
                    return false;
                }
            }

            uncomp_size = file_ofs;
            comp_size = state.m_comp_size;
            cur_archive_file_ofs = state.m_cur_archive_file_ofs;
        }
    }

    // Write data descriptor if needed
    if (level_and_flags & MZ_ZIP_FLAG_WRITE_HEADER_SET_SIZE) == 0 {
        let mut local_dir_footer = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE64];
        let mut local_dir_footer_size = MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE32;

        // Write descriptor ID
        local_dir_footer[0..4].copy_from_slice(&MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID.to_le_bytes());
        
        // Write CRC32
        local_dir_footer[4..8].copy_from_slice(&uncomp_crc32.to_le_bytes());
        
        // Write sizes
        if p_extra_data.is_none() {
            if comp_size > u32::MAX as u64 {
                mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_ARCHIVE_TOO_LARGE);
                return false;
            }
            
            local_dir_footer[8..12].copy_from_slice(&(comp_size as u32).to_le_bytes());
            local_dir_footer[12..16].copy_from_slice(&(uncomp_size as u32).to_le_bytes());
        } else {
            local_dir_footer[8..16].copy_from_slice(&comp_size.to_le_bytes());
            local_dir_footer[16..24].copy_from_slice(&uncomp_size.to_le_bytes());
            local_dir_footer_size = MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTER_SIZE64;
        }

        if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_file_ofs, &local_dir_footer[..local_dir_footer_size]) {
            return false;
        }
        cur_archive_file_ofs += local_dir_footer_size as u64;
    } else {
        // Update local header with actual sizes if needed
        if p_extra_data.is_some() {
            let uncomp_size_ptr = if uncomp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut uncomp_size) } else { None };
            let comp_size_ptr = if comp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut comp_size) } else { None };
            let local_header_ofs_ptr = if local_dir_header_ofs >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None };
            
            extra_size = mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
                &mut extra_data,
                uncomp_size_ptr,
                comp_size_ptr,
                local_header_ofs_ptr,
            ) as u16;
        }

        // Recreate local header with actual sizes
        let uncomp_size_32 = if uncomp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { u32::MAX } else { uncomp_size as u32 };
        let comp_size_32 = if comp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { u32::MAX } else { comp_size as u32 };
        
        if !mz_zip_writer_create_local_dir_header(
            pzip,
            &mut local_dir_header,
            archive_name_size as u16,
            extra_size + user_extra_data_len as u16,
            uncomp_size_32,
            comp_size_32,
            uncomp_crc32,
            method,
            gen_flags,
            dos_time,
            dos_date,
        ) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_INTERNAL_ERROR);
            return false;
        }

        // Write updated header
        let mut cur_archive_header_file_ofs = local_dir_header_ofs;
        if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_header_file_ofs, &local_dir_header) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
            return false;
        }

        // If we have extra data, also need to write the extra data block again
        if p_extra_data.is_some() {
            cur_archive_header_file_ofs += local_dir_header.len() as u64;
            
            // Write archive name
            if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_header_file_ofs, p_archive_name.as_bytes()) {
                mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
                return false;
            }
            cur_archive_header_file_ofs += archive_name_size as u64;
            
            // Write extra data
            let extra_slice = &extra_data[..extra_size as usize];
            if !write_to_zip(pzip, cur_archive_header_file_ofs, extra_slice) {
                mz_zip_set_error(pzip as *const _ as usize, MZ_ZIP_FILE_WRITE_FAILED);
                return false;
            }
        }
    }

    // Update central directory
    if p_extra_data.is_some() {
        let uncomp_size_ptr = if uncomp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut uncomp_size) } else { None };
        let comp_size_ptr = if comp_size >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut comp_size) } else { None };
        let local_header_ofs_ptr = if local_dir_header_ofs >= u32::MAX as u64 { Some(&mut local_dir_header_ofs) } else { None };
        
        extra_size = mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
            &mut extra_data,
            uncomp_size_ptr,
            comp_size_ptr,
            local_header_ofs_ptr,
        ) as u16;
    }

    let success = mz_zip_writer_add_to_central_dir(
        pzip,
        p_archive_name,
        archive_name_size as u16,
        p_extra_data,
        extra_size,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        uncomp_size,
        comp_size,
        uncomp_crc32,
        method,
        gen_flags,
        dos_time,
        dos_date,
        local_dir_header_ofs,
        0, // ext_attributes
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    );

    if success {
        pzip.m_total_files += 1;
        pzip.m_archive_size = cur_archive_file_ofs;
    }

    success
}

/// Read function for stdio files
/// 
/// This function reads from a file at a specific offset, similar to pread().
/// It seeks to the requested offset if not already there, then reads the data.
pub fn mz_file_read_func_stdio(p_opaque: &mut File, file_ofs: u64, p_buf: &mut [u8]) -> usize {
    // Check if we need to seek
    let current_pos = match p_opaque.stream_position() {
        Ok(pos) => pos,
        Err(_) => return 0,
    };

    if current_pos != file_ofs {
        if let Err(_) = p_opaque.seek(SeekFrom::Start(file_ofs)) {
            return 0;
        }
    }

    match p_opaque.read(p_buf) {
        Ok(bytes_read) => bytes_read,
        Err(_) => 0,
    }
}

/// Add a file to a ZIP archive from a C FILE handle
/// 
/// This is a convenience wrapper around `mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback`
/// that uses stdio file reading.
pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
    pzip: &mut MzZipArchive,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_src_file: &mut File,
    max_size: u64,
    p_file_time: Option<&MZ_TIME_T>,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    user_extra_data: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_len: u32,
    user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Create a closure that captures the file
    let read_callback = |opaque: &mut dyn std::any::Any, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]| -> usize {
        if let Some(file) = opaque.downcast_mut::<File>() {
            mz_file_read_func_stdio(file, file_ofs, buf)
        } else {
            0
        }
    };

    mz_zip_writer_add_read_buf_callback(
        pzip,
        p_archive_name,
        read_callback,
        p_src_file,
        max_size,
        p_file_time,
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
        user_extra_data,
        user_extra_data_len,
        user_extra_data_central,
        user_extra_data_central_len,
    )
}

// Helper function to write data to ZIP archive
fn write_to_zip(pzip: &MzZipArchive, offset: u64, data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    // This would use the zip archive's write callback
    // For now, we assume it exists and returns success
    true
}

// Helper struct for deflate compression (simplified)
struct TdeflCompressor<F>
where
    F: Fn(&[u8], &mut MzZipWriterAddState) -> bool,
{
    _callback: F,
    _level: u32,
}

impl<F> TdeflCompressor<F>
where
    F: Fn(&[u8], &mut MzZipWriterAddState) -> bool,
{
    fn new(level: u32, callback: F) -> Option<Self> {
        Some(Self {
            _callback: callback,
            _level: level,
        })
    }

    fn compress(&mut self, data: &[u8], state: &mut MzZipWriterAddState) -> bool {
        // Simplified compression - in real code this would use flate2 or similar
        (self._callback)(data, state)
    }

    fn finish(&mut self, state: &mut MzZipWriterAddState) -> bool {
        // Finish compression
        true
    }
}

type mz_file_read_func = fn(&mut dyn std::any::Any, u64, &mut [u8]) -> usize;

// Placeholder structs (would be defined in other modules)

fn mz_zip_writer_validate_archive_name(_name: &str) -> bool { true }
fn mz_zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(_pzip: &MzZipArchive) -> u32 { 0 }
fn mz_zip_writer_write_zeros(_pzip: &MzZipArchive, _offset: u64, _count: u32) -> bool { true }
fn mz_zip_time_t_to_dos_time(_time: MZ_TIME_T, _dos_time: &mut u16, _dos_date: &mut u16) {}
fn mz_zip_writer_create_zip64_extra_data(
    _data: &mut [u8],
    _uncomp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    _comp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    _local_header_ofs: Option<&mut u64>,
) -> u32 { 0 }

    _pzip: &MzZipArchive,
    _header: &mut [u8],
    _name_len: u16,
    _extra_len: u16,
    _uncomp_size: u32,

    _comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    _comment_size: u16,
    _uncomp_size: u64,
    _comp_size: u64,
    _crc32: u32,

    _dos_date: u16,

    _ext_attrs: u32,
    _user_extra_data_central: Option<&[u8]>,
    _user_extra_data_central_len: u32,
) -> bool { true }

// Type alias for time - matches C's MZ_TIME_T which would be i64
type MzTimeT = i64;

pub fn mz_zip_writer_add_file(
    pzip: usize,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_src_filename: &str,
    p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    comment_size: u16,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> u8 {
    let mut uncomp_size = 0u64;
    let mut file_modified_time: Option<MzTimeT> = None;

    // Get file modified time if supported
    #[cfg(not(feature = "miniz_no_time"))]
    #[cfg(not(feature = "miniz_no_stdio"))]
    {
        if let Ok(metadata) = std::fs::metadata(p_src_filename) {
            if let Ok(modified) = metadata.modified() {
                if let Ok(duration) = modified.duration_since(std::time::UNIX_EPOCH) {
                    file_modified_time = Some(duration.as_secs() as MzTimeT);
                }
            }
        }
        if file_modified_time.is_none() {
            // Could not get file time
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 9); // MZ_ZIP_FILE_STAT_FAILED
            return 0;
        }
    }

    // Open source file
    let p_src_file = match File::open(p_src_filename) {
        Ok(file) => file,
        Err(_) => {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 10); // MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED
            return 0;
        }
    };

    // Get file size
    if let Ok(metadata) = std::fs::metadata(p_src_filename) {
        uncomp_size = metadata.len();
    } else {
        // Fallback: seek to end to get size
        let mut file = p_src_file.try_clone().expect("Failed to clone file");
        if let (Ok(pos), Ok(_)) = (file.seek(SeekFrom::End(0)), file.seek(SeekFrom::Start(0))) {
            uncomp_size = pos;
        } else {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 10); // MZ_ZIP_FILE_OPEN_FAILED
            return 0;
        }
    }

    // Call the cfile version
    let status = mz_zip_writer_add_cfile(
        pzip,
        p_archive_name,
        Box::new(p_src_file),
        uncomp_size,
        file_modified_time.as_ref(),
        p_comment,
        comment_size,
        level_and_flags,
    );

    status
}

fn mz_zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
    pzip: usize,
    p_new_ext: &mut MzZipArray,
    p_ext: Option<&[u8]>,
    ext_len: u32,
    p_comp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    p_uncomp_size: Option<&mut u64>,
    p_local_header_ofs: Option<&mut u64>,
    p_disk_start: Option<&mut u32>,
) -> u8 {
    // Reserve space (+ 64 should be enough for any new zip64 data)
    let new_capacity = ext_len as usize + 64;
    
    // Assuming mz_zip_array_reserve exists and returns bool
    if !mz_zip_array_reserve(pzip, p_new_ext, new_capacity, false) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 12); // MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED
        return 0;
    }

    // Resize to 0 (clear the array)
    if !mz_zip_array_resize(pzip, p_new_ext, 0, false) {
        mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 12); // MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED
        return 0;
    }

    // If we have any zip64 fields to write
    if p_uncomp_size.is_some() || p_comp_size.is_some() || p_local_header_ofs.is_some() || p_disk_start.is_some() {
        let mut new_ext_block = [0u8; 64];
        let mut p_dst = 0usize;
        
        // Write header ID
        new_ext_block[p_dst..p_dst + 2].copy_from_slice(&(MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as u16).to_le_bytes());
        p_dst += 2;
        
        // Skip size for now - we'll fill it later
        let size_offset = p_dst;
        p_dst += 2;
        
        let start_pos = p_dst;
        
        // Write uncompressed size if provided
        if let Some(uncomp_size) = p_uncomp_size {
            new_ext_block[p_dst..p_dst + 8].copy_from_slice(&uncomp_size.to_le_bytes());
            p_dst += 8;
        }
        
        // Write compressed size if provided
        if let Some(comp_size) = p_comp_size {
            new_ext_block[p_dst..p_dst + 8].copy_from_slice(&comp_size.to_le_bytes());
            p_dst += 8;
        }
        
        // Write local header offset if provided
        if let Some(local_header_ofs) = p_local_header_ofs {
            new_ext_block[p_dst..p_dst + 8].copy_from_slice(&local_header_ofs.to_le_bytes());
            p_dst += 8;
        }
        
        // Write disk start if provided
        if let Some(disk_start) = p_disk_start {
            new_ext_block[p_dst..p_dst + 4].copy_from_slice(&disk_start.to_le_bytes());
            p_dst += 4;
        }
        
        // Calculate and write size
        let data_size = p_dst - start_pos;
        new_ext_block[size_offset..size_offset + 2].copy_from_slice(&(data_size as u16).to_le_bytes());
        
        // Push the block to the array
        if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pzip, p_new_ext, &new_ext_block[0..p_dst]) {
            mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 12); // MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED
            return 0;
        }
    }
    
    // Process existing extra fields
    if let Some(p_ext) = p_ext {
        let mut extra_data = p_ext;
        let mut remaining = ext_len;
        
        while remaining > 0 {
            if remaining < 4 {
                mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 2); // MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED
                return 0;
            }
            
            let field_id = u16::from_le_bytes([extra_data[0], extra_data[1]]);
            let field_data_size = u16::from_le_bytes([extra_data[2], extra_data[3]]);
            let field_total_size = field_data_size as u32 + 4;
            
            if field_total_size > remaining {
                mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 2); // MZ_ZIP_INVALID_HEADER_OR_CORRUPTED
                return 0;
            }
            
            // Skip zip64 extended info field (we already wrote it)
            if field_id != MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID as u16 {
                if !mz_zip_array_push_back(pzip, p_new_ext, &extra_data[0..field_total_size as usize]) {
                    mz_zip_set_error(pzip, 12); // MZ_ZIP_ALLOC_FAILED
                    return 0;
                }
            }
            
            // Move to next field
            extra_data = &extra_data[field_total_size as usize..];
            remaining -= field_total_size;
        }
    }
    
    1 // MZ_TRUE
}

    // Implementation would resize the underlying storage
    true
}

fn mz_zip_array_resize(_pzip: usize, _array: &mut MzZipArray, _new_size: usize, _growing: bool) -> bool {
    // Implementation would resize the array
    true
}

fn mz_zip_array_push_back(_pzip: usize, _array: &mut MzZipArray, _data: &[u8]) -> bool {
    // Implementation would append data to the array
    true
}

    _pzip: usize,
    _p_archive_name: &str,
    _p_src_file: Box<dyn Read>,
    _max_size: u64,
    _p_file_time: Option<&MzTimeT>,
    _p_comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    _comment_size: u16,

    mem::{self, size_of},
    ptr, slice,

    sync::OnceLock,

// Central directory header record offsets

// Local directory header offsets

// End of central directory offsets

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum ZipMode {
    Invalid,
    Reading,
    Writing,
    WritingHasBeenFinalized,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]

// ============================================================================
// Core data structures
// ============================================================================

#[derive(Debug)]

impl ZipArray {
    pub fn new(element_size: usize) -> Self {
        Self {
            data: Vec::new(),
            element_size,
        }
    }

    pub fn size(&self) -> usize {
        self.data.len()
    }

    pub fn clear(&mut self) {
        self.data.clear();
    }

    pub fn resize(&mut self, new_size: usize, zero_new: bool) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        let old_len = self.data.len();
        if new_size > old_len {
            self.data.reserve(new_size - old_len);
            if zero_new {
                self.data.resize(new_size, 0);
            } else {
                unsafe {
                    self.data.set_len(new_size);
                }
            }
        } else if new_size < old_len {
            self.data.truncate(new_size);
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn push_back(&mut self, data: &[u8]) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        if data.len() % self.element_size != 0 {
            return Err(ZipError::InternalError);
        }
        self.data.extend_from_slice(data);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn as_slice(&self) -> &[u8] {
        &self.data
    }

    pub fn as_mut_slice(&mut self) -> &mut [u8] {
        &mut self.data
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]

impl ZipInternalState {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            central_dir: ZipArray::new(1),
            central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(size_of::<u32>()),
            sorted_central_dir_offsets: ZipArray::new(size_of::<u32>()),
            init_flags: 0,
            zip64: false,
            zip64_has_extended_info_fields: false,
            file_archive_start_ofs: 0,
            mem: Vec::new(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug)]

pub trait ZipIo {
    fn read(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>;
    fn write(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError>;
    fn tell(&self) -> Result<u64, ZipError>;
    fn seek(&mut self, offset: u64) -> Result<(), ZipError>;
}

pub type ZipAllocFunc = fn(opaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void, items: usize, size: usize) -> *mut std::ffi::c_void;
pub type ZipFreeFunc = fn(opaque: *mut std::ffi::c_void, address: *mut std::ffi::c_void);

#[derive(Debug)]

impl ZipArchive {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            state: None,
            zip_mode: ZipMode::Invalid,
            total_files: 0,
            archive_size: 0,
            central_directory_file_ofs: 0,
            file_offset_alignment: 0,
            io_opaque: ptr::null_mut(),
            read_func: None,
            write_func: None,
            alloc_opaque: ptr::null_mut(),
            alloc_func: None,
            free_func: None,
            last_error: ZipError::Ok,
        }
    }

    fn set_error(&mut self, err: ZipError) -> bool {
        self.last_error = err;
        false
    }

    fn alloc(&mut self, items: usize, size: usize) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        if let Some(alloc_func) = self.alloc_func {
            unsafe {
                let ptr = alloc_func(self.alloc_opaque, items, size);
                if ptr.is_null() {
                    return None;
                }
                let total_size = items * size;
                Some(Vec::from_raw_parts(ptr as *mut u8, total_size, total_size))
            }
        } else {
            // Default allocation
            let total_size = items * size;
            let mut vec = Vec::with_capacity(total_size);
            unsafe { vec.set_len(total_size) };
            Some(vec)
        }
    }

    fn free(&mut self, buf: Vec<u8>) {
        if let Some(free_func) = self.free_func {
            unsafe {
                let ptr = buf.as_ptr() as *mut std::ffi::c_void;
                mem::forget(buf); // Prevent double free
                free_func(self.alloc_opaque, ptr);
            }
        }
        // Else: Vec will be dropped normally
    }

    fn read_bytes(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError> {
        if let Some(read_func) = self.read_func {
            let bytes_read = unsafe {
                read_func(self.io_opaque, file_ofs, buf.as_mut_ptr() as *mut std::ffi::c_void, buf.len())
            };
            if bytes_read == buf.len() {
                Ok(bytes_read)
            } else {
                Err(ZipError::FileReadFailed)
            }
        } else {
            Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter)
        }
    }

    fn write_bytes(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<usize, ZipError> {
        if let Some(write_func) = self.write_func {
            let bytes_written = unsafe {
                write_func(self.io_opaque, file_ofs, buf.as_ptr() as *const std::ffi::c_void, buf.len())
            };
            if bytes_written == buf.len() {
                Ok(bytes_written)
            } else {
                Err(ZipError::FileWriteFailed)
            }
        } else {
            Err(ZipError::InvalidParameter)
        }
    }

    fn read_exact(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &mut [u8]) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        self.read_bytes(file_ofs, buf)?;
        Ok(())
    }

    fn write_exact(&mut self, file_ofs: u64, buf: &[u8]) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
        self.write_bytes(file_ofs, buf)?;
        Ok(())
    }
}

// ============================================================================
// Helper functions
// ============================================================================

fn read_le_u16(data: &[u8]) -> u16 {
    u16::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1]])
}

fn read_le_u32(data: &[u8]) -> u32 {
    u32::from_le_bytes([data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3]])
}

fn read_le_u64(data: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    u64::from_le_bytes([
        data[0], data[1], data[2], data[3],
        data[4], data[5], data[6], data[7],
    ])
}

fn write_le_u16(data: &mut [u8], value: u16) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[0..2].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_le_u32(data: &mut [u8], value: u32) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[0..4].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn write_le_u64(data: &mut [u8], value: u64) {
    let bytes = value.to_le_bytes();
    data[0..8].copy_from_slice(&bytes);
}

fn zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(zip: &ZipArchive) -> u32 {
    if zip.file_offset_alignment == 0 {
        return 0;
    }
    
    let padding_needed = (zip.file_offset_alignment - (zip.archive_size % zip.file_offset_alignment as u64)) as u32;
    if padding_needed == zip.file_offset_alignment {
        0
    } else {
        padding_needed
    }
}

fn zip_writer_write_zeros(zip: &mut ZipArchive, file_ofs: u64, n: u32) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
    if n == 0 {
        return Ok(());
    }
    
    const ZERO_BUF_SIZE: usize = 4096;
    let zero_buf = vec![0u8; ZERO_BUF_SIZE];
    
    let mut bytes_remaining = n as usize;
    let mut current_ofs = file_ofs;
    
    while bytes_remaining > 0 {
        let to_write = cmp::min(bytes_remaining, ZERO_BUF_SIZE);
        zip.write_exact(current_ofs, &zero_buf[..to_write])?;
        current_ofs += to_write as u64;
        bytes_remaining -= to_write;
    }
    
    Ok(())
}

fn zip_file_stat_internal(
    source_zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    file_index: u32,
    central_header: &[u8],
    stat: &mut ZipArchiveFileStat,
) -> Result<(), ZipError> {
    if central_header.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    if read_le_u32(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    stat.file_index = file_index;
    stat.central_dir_ofs = 0; // Not used in this context
    stat.method = read_le_u16(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_METHOD_OFS..]);
    stat.bit_flag = read_le_u16(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    stat.crc32 = read_le_u32(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_CRC32_OFS..]);
    
    let compressed_size_32 = read_le_u32(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]);
    let uncompressed_size_32 = read_le_u32(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]);
    let local_header_ofs_32 = read_le_u32(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..]);
    
    stat.compressed_size_32 = compressed_size_32;
    stat.uncompressed_size_32 = uncompressed_size_32;
    stat.local_header_ofs = local_header_ofs_32 as u64;
    
    // Handle zip64 extended information
    stat.compressed_size_64 = compressed_size_32 as u64;
    stat.uncompressed_size_64 = uncompressed_size_32 as u64;
    
    let filename_len = read_le_u16(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let extra_len = read_le_u16(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    let comment_len = read_le_u16(&central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as usize;
    
    let extra_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + filename_len;
    let extra_end = extra_start + extra_len;
    
    if central_header.len() < extra_end {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    let extra_data = &central_header[extra_start..extra_end];
    
    // Parse extra field for zip64 information
    let mut offset = 0;
    while offset + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
        let header_id = read_le_u16(&extra_data[offset..]);
        let data_size = read_le_u16(&extra_data[offset + 2..]) as usize;
        
        if offset + 4 + data_size > extra_data.len() {
            break;
        }
        
        if header_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
            let data = &extra_data[offset + 4..offset + 4 + data_size];
            let mut data_offset = 0;
            
            if uncompressed_size_32 == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_offset + 8 <= data.len() {
                stat.uncompressed_size_64 = read_le_u64(&data[data_offset..]);
                data_offset += 8;
            }
            
            if compressed_size_32 == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_offset + 8 <= data.len() {
                stat.compressed_size_64 = read_le_u64(&data[data_offset..]);
                data_offset += 8;
            }
            
            if local_header_ofs_32 == 0xFFFFFFFF && data_offset + 8 <= data.len() {
                stat.local_header_ofs = read_le_u64(&data[data_offset..]);
            }
            
            break;
        }
        
        offset += 4 + data_size;
    }
    
    stat.compressed_size = stat.compressed_size_64;
    stat.uncompressed_size = stat.uncompressed_size_64;
    
    // Read filename
    let filename_start = MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE;
    let filename_end = filename_start + filename_len;
    
    if central_header.len() < filename_end {
        return Err(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    let filename_bytes = &central_header[filename_start..filename_end];
    stat.file_name = String::from_utf8_lossy(filename_bytes).into_owned();
    
    // Set flags
    stat.is_directory = stat.file_name.ends_with('/');
    stat.is_encrypted = (stat.bit_flag & 1) != 0;
    stat.is_supported = true;
    
    Ok(())
}

fn zip_get_cdh(zip: &ZipArchive, file_index: u32) -> Option<&[u8]> {
    let state = zip.state.as_ref()?;
    
    if file_index as usize >= state.central_dir_offsets.size() / size_of::<u32>() {
        return None;
    }
    
    let offsets_slice = state.central_dir_offsets.as_slice();
    let offset = read_le_u32(&offsets_slice[file_index as usize * size_of::<u32>()..]) as usize;
    
    if offset + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE <= state.central_dir.size() {
        Some(&state.central_dir.as_slice()[offset..])
    } else {
        None
    }
}

fn zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
    new_ext_block: &mut ZipArray,
    zip: &ZipArchive,
    src_ext: &[u8],
    src_ext_len: usize,
    comp_size: &u64,
    uncomp_size: &u64,
    local_header_ofs: &u64,
    disk_start: Option<&u32>,
) -> bool {
    // This is a simplified implementation
    // In reality, we need to parse the existing extra field and update/replace zip64 fields
    let mut data = Vec::new();
    
    // Write zip64 extended information field header
    data.extend_from_slice(&MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID.to_le_bytes());
    
    // Calculate data size (we'll write uncompressed size, compressed size, and local header offset)
    let mut field_data = Vec::new();
    write_le_u64(&mut field_data, *uncomp_size);
    write_le_u64(&mut field_data, *comp_size);
    write_le_u64(&mut field_data, *local_header_ofs);
    
    if let Some(disk_start) = disk_start {
        write_le_u32(&mut field_data, *disk_start);
    }
    
    // Write data size
    let data_size = field_data.len() as u16;
    data.extend_from_slice(&data_size.to_le_bytes());
    data.extend_from_slice(&field_data);
    
    new_ext_block.push_back(&data).is_ok()
}

// ============================================================================
// Main functions
// ============================================================================

pub fn zip_writer_add_from_zip_reader(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    source_zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    src_file_index: u32,
) -> bool {
    // Sanity checks
    if zip.state.is_none() || zip.zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing || source_zip.read_func.is_none() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let state = zip.state.as_mut().unwrap();
    
    // Don't support copying from zip64 to non-zip64
    if source_zip.state.as_ref().unwrap().zip64 && !state.zip64 {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Get pointer to source central dir header
    let src_central_header = match zip_get_cdh(source_zip, src_file_index) {
        Some(header) => header,
        None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter),
    };
    
    if src_central_header.len() < MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    if read_le_u32(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    let src_filename_len = read_le_u16(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as u32;
    let src_comment_len = read_le_u16(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMMENT_LEN_OFS..]) as u32;
    let src_ext_len = read_le_u16(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as u32;
    let src_central_dir_following_data_size = src_filename_len + src_ext_len + src_comment_len;
    
    // Check central directory size limit
    if (state.central_dir.size() + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size as usize + 32) >= usize::MAX {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }
    
    let num_alignment_padding_bytes = zip_writer_compute_padding_needed_for_file_alignment(zip);
    
    // Check file count limits
    if !state.zip64 {
        if zip.total_files == MZ_UINT16_MAX {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else if zip.total_files == u32::MAX {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
    }
    
    // Get source file stat
    let mut src_file_stat = ZipArchiveFileStat {
        file_index: 0,
        central_dir_ofs: 0,
        file_name: String::new(),
        uncompressed_size: 0,
        compressed_size: 0,
        uncompressed_size_32: 0,
        compressed_size_32: 0,
        uncompressed_size_64: 0,
        compressed_size_64: 0,
        local_header_ofs: 0,
        crc32: 0,
        method: 0,
        bit_flag: 0,
        version_made_by: 0,
        version_needed: 0,
        time: 0,
        date: 0,
        external_attr: 0,
        is_directory: false,
        is_encrypted: false,
        is_supported: false,
    };
    
    if zip_file_stat_internal(source_zip, src_file_index, src_central_header, &mut src_file_stat).is_err() {
        return false;
    }
    
    let mut cur_src_file_ofs = src_file_stat.local_header_ofs;
    let mut cur_dst_file_ofs = zip.archive_size;
    
    // Read source archive's local dir header
    let mut local_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    if source_zip.read_exact(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut local_header).is_err() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
    }
    
    if read_le_u32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_SIG_OFS..]) != MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidHeaderOrCorrupted);
    }
    
    cur_src_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    
    // Compute total size to copy
    let local_header_filename_size = read_le_u16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS..]) as u32;
    let local_header_extra_len = read_le_u16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..]) as u32;
    let mut local_header_comp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    let mut local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u32(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..]) as u64;
    
    let mut src_archive_bytes_remaining = src_file_stat.compressed_size + local_header_filename_size as u64 + local_header_extra_len as u64;
    
    // Check for zip64 extended information in local header
    let mut found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = false;
    if local_header_extra_len > 0 && (local_header_comp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX || local_header_uncomp_size == MZ_UINT32_MAX) {
        let mut extra_data = vec![0u8; local_header_extra_len as usize];
        if source_zip.read_exact(
            src_file_stat.local_header_ofs + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64 + local_header_filename_size as u64,
            &mut extra_data,
        ).is_err() {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
        }
        
        let mut extra_offset = 0;
        while extra_offset + 4 <= extra_data.len() {
            let field_id = read_le_u16(&extra_data[extra_offset..]);
            let field_data_size = read_le_u16(&extra_data[extra_offset + 2..]) as usize;
            let field_total_size = 4 + field_data_size;
            
            if extra_offset + field_total_size > extra_data.len() {
                break;
            }
            
            if field_id == MZ_ZIP64_EXTENDED_INFORMATION_FIELD_HEADER_ID {
                if field_data_size >= 16 {
                    let field_data = &extra_data[extra_offset + 4..extra_offset + 4 + field_data_size];
                    local_header_uncomp_size = read_le_u64(&field_data[0..8]);
                    local_header_comp_size = read_le_u64(&field_data[8..16]);
                    found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir = true;
                    break;
                }
            }
            
            extra_offset += field_total_size;
        }
    }
    
    // Check archive size for non-zip64
    if !state.zip64 {
        let approx_new_archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs
            + num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_archive_bytes_remaining
            + (size_of::<u32>() * 4) as u64
            + state.central_dir.size() as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + src_central_dir_following_data_size as u64
            + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64
            + 64;
        
        if approx_new_archive_size >= MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
    }
    
    // Write padding
    if zip_writer_write_zeros(zip, cur_dst_file_ofs, num_alignment_padding_bytes).is_err() {
        return false;
    }
    
    cur_dst_file_ofs += num_alignment_padding_bytes as u64;
    let local_dir_header_ofs = cur_dst_file_ofs;
    
    // Copy local header
    if zip.write_exact(cur_dst_file_ofs, &local_header).is_err() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }
    
    cur_dst_file_ofs += MZ_ZIP_LOCAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    
    // Allocate buffer for copying data
    let buf_size = cmp::max(32, cmp::min(MZ_ZIP_MAX_IO_BUF_SIZE, src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize));
    let mut buf = match zip.alloc(1, buf_size) {
        Some(buf) => buf,
        None => return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed),
    };
    
    // Copy the file data
    while src_archive_bytes_remaining > 0 {
        let to_copy = cmp::min(buf.len(), src_archive_bytes_remaining as usize);
        
        if source_zip.read_exact(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut buf[..to_copy]).is_err() {
            zip.free(buf);
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
        }
        
        if zip.write_exact(cur_dst_file_ofs, &buf[..to_copy]).is_err() {
            zip.free(buf);
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }
        
        cur_src_file_ofs += to_copy as u64;
        cur_dst_file_ofs += to_copy as u64;
        src_archive_bytes_remaining -= to_copy as u64;
    }
    
    // Handle data descriptor if present
    let bit_flags = read_le_u16(&local_header[MZ_ZIP_LDH_BIT_FLAG_OFS..]);
    if (bit_flags & 8) != 0 {
        let source_state = source_zip.state.as_ref().unwrap();
        
        if source_state.zip64 || found_zip64_ext_data_in_ldir {
            // Copy zip64 data descriptor
            let mut data_desc = [0u8; 24];
            if source_zip.read_exact(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut data_desc).is_err() {
                zip.free(buf);
                return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            }
            
            let n = if read_le_u32(&data_desc) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID {
                24
            } else {
                20
            };
            
            if zip.write_exact(cur_dst_file_ofs, &data_desc[..n]).is_err() {
                zip.free(buf);
                return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
            }
            
            cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
            cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
        } else {
            // Handle non-zip64 to zip64 conversion if needed
            let mut data_desc = [0u8; 16];
            if source_zip.read_exact(cur_src_file_ofs, &mut data_desc[..16]).is_err() {
                zip.free(buf);
                return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileReadFailed);
            }
            
            let has_id = read_le_u32(&data_desc) == MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID;
            
            if state.zip64 {
                // Convert to zip64
                let src_offset = if has_id { 4 } else { 0 };
                let src_crc32 = read_le_u32(&data_desc[src_offset..]);
                let src_comp_size = read_le_u32(&data_desc[src_offset + 4..]) as u64;
                let src_uncomp_size = read_le_u32(&data_desc[src_offset + 8..]) as u64;
                
                let mut zip64_desc = [0u8; 24];
                write_le_u32(&mut zip64_desc, MZ_ZIP_DATA_DESCRIPTOR_ID);
                write_le_u32(&mut zip64_desc[4..], src_crc32);
                write_le_u64(&mut zip64_desc[8..], src_comp_size);
                write_le_u64(&mut zip64_desc[16..], src_uncomp_size);
                
                if zip.write_exact(cur_dst_file_ofs, &zip64_desc).is_err() {
                    zip.free(buf);
                    return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
                
                cur_src_file_ofs += (if has_id { 16 } else { 12 }) as u64;
                cur_dst_file_ofs += 24;
            } else {
                // Copy as-is
                let n = if has_id { 16 } else { 12 };
                if zip.write_exact(cur_dst_file_ofs, &data_desc[..n]).is_err() {
                    zip.free(buf);
                    return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
                }
                
                cur_src_file_ofs += n as u64;
                cur_dst_file_ofs += n as u64;
            }
        }
    }
    
    zip.free(buf);
    
    // Add new central directory header
    let orig_central_dir_size = state.central_dir.size();
    let mut new_central_header = [0u8; MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE];
    new_central_header[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE].copy_from_slice(&src_central_header[..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]);
    
    if state.zip64 {
        // Update for zip64
        let src_ext = &src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize..];
        let mut new_ext_block = ZipArray::new(1);
        
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_COMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], u32::MAX);
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_DECOMPRESSED_SIZE_OFS..], u32::MAX);
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..], u32::MAX);
        
        if !zip_writer_update_zip64_extension_block(
            &mut new_ext_block,
            zip,
            src_ext,
            src_ext_len as usize,
            &src_file_stat.compressed_size,
            &src_file_stat.uncompressed_size,
            &local_dir_header_ofs,
            None,
        ) {
            return false;
        }
        
        write_le_u16(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_EXTRA_LEN_OFS..], new_ext_block.size() as u16);
        
        // Push all parts to central directory
        if state.central_dir.push_back(&new_central_header).is_err()
            || state.central_dir.push_back(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize]).is_err()
            || state.central_dir.push_back(new_ext_block.as_slice()).is_err()
            || state.central_dir.push_back(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_filename_len as usize + src_ext_len as usize..]).is_err()
        {
            state.central_dir.resize(orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }
    } else {
        // Non-zip64
        if local_dir_header_ofs >= MZ_UINT32_MAX || cur_dst_file_ofs > MZ_UINT32_MAX {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::ArchiveTooLarge);
        }
        
        write_le_u32(&mut new_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CDH_LOCAL_HEADER_OFS..], local_dir_header_ofs as u32);
        
        if state.central_dir.push_back(&new_central_header).is_err()
            || state.central_dir.push_back(&src_central_header[MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE..MZ_ZIP_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE + src_central_dir_following_data_size as usize]).is_err()
        {
            state.central_dir.resize(orig_central_dir_size, false);
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
        }
    }
    
    // Check central directory size
    if state.central_dir.size() >= usize::MAX {
        state.central_dir.resize(orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::UnsupportedCdirSize);
    }
    
    // Add offset to offsets array
    let offset_bytes = (orig_central_dir_size as u32).to_le_bytes();
    if state.central_dir_offsets.push_back(&offset_bytes).is_err() {
        state.central_dir.resize(orig_central_dir_size, false);
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::AllocFailed);
    }
    
    zip.total_files += 1;
    zip.archive_size = cur_dst_file_ofs;
    
    true
}

pub fn zip_writer_finalize_archive(zip: &mut ZipArchive) -> bool {
    if zip.state.is_none() || zip.zip_mode != ZipMode::Writing {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    let state = zip.state.as_mut().unwrap();
    
    // Check limits
    if state.zip64 {
        if state.central_dir.size() >= usize::MAX {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    } else {
        if zip.total_files > MZ_UINT16_MAX
            || (zip.archive_size + state.central_dir.size() as u64 + MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64) > MZ_UINT32_MAX
        {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::TooManyFiles);
        }
    }
    
    let mut central_dir_ofs = 0u64;
    let mut central_dir_size = 0u64;
    
    if zip.total_files > 0 {
        // Write central directory
        central_dir_ofs = zip.archive_size;
        central_dir_size = state.central_dir.size() as u64;
        zip.central_directory_file_ofs = central_dir_ofs;
        
        if zip.write_exact(central_dir_ofs, state.central_dir.as_slice()).is_err() {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }
        
        zip.archive_size += central_dir_size;
    }
    
    let mut hdr = [0u8; 256];
    
    if state.zip64 {
        // Write zip64 end of central directory header
        let rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr = zip.archive_size;
        
        write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_SIZE_OF_RECORD_OFS..], (MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE - 12) as u64);
        write_le_u16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_MADE_BY_OFS..], 0x031E);
        write_le_u16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_VERSION_NEEDED_OFS..], 0x002D);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..], zip.total_files as u64);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..], zip.total_files as u64);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..], central_dir_size);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..], central_dir_ofs);
        
        if zip.write_exact(zip.archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]).is_err() {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }
        
        zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
        
        // Write zip64 end of central directory locator
        hdr.fill(0);
        write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIG);
        write_le_u64(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_REL_OFS_TO_ZIP64_ECDR_OFS..], rel_ofs_to_zip64_ecdr);
        write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP64_ECDL_TOTAL_NUMBER_OF_DISKS_OFS..], 1);
        
        if zip.write_exact(zip.archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE]).is_err() {
            return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
        }
        
        zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP64_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_LOCATOR_SIZE as u64;
    }
    
    // Write end of central directory record
    hdr.fill(0);
    write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_SIG_OFS..], MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIG);
    
    let total_files_16 = cmp::min(zip.total_files, MZ_UINT16_MAX);
    write_le_u16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_NUM_ENTRIES_ON_DISK_OFS..], total_files_16 as u16);
    write_le_u16(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_TOTAL_ENTRIES_OFS..], total_files_16 as u16);
    
    let central_dir_size_32 = cmp::min(central_dir_size, MZ_UINT32_MAX) as u32;
    write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_SIZE_OFS..], central_dir_size_32);
    
    let central_dir_ofs_32 = cmp::min(central_dir_ofs, MZ_UINT32_MAX) as u32;
    write_le_u32(&mut hdr[MZ_ZIP_ECDH_CDIR_OFS_OFS..], central_dir_ofs_32);
    
    if zip.write_exact(zip.archive_size, &hdr[..MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE]).is_err() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::FileWriteFailed);
    }
    
    zip.archive_size += MZ_ZIP_END_OF_CENTRAL_DIR_HEADER_SIZE as u64;
    zip.zip_mode = ZipMode::WritingHasBeenFinalized;
    
    true
}

pub fn zip_writer_finalize_heap_archive(
    zip: &mut ZipArchive,
    buf_out: &mut Option<Vec<u8>>,
    size_out: &mut usize,
) -> bool {
    if buf_out.is_none() || size_out.is_none() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    *buf_out = None;
    *size_out = 0;
    
    if zip.state.is_none() {
        return zip.set_error(ZipError::InvalidParameter);
    }
    
    // Check if using heap write function (simplified)
    if !zip_writer_finalize_archive(zip) {
        return false;
    }
    
    let state = zip.state.as_mut().unwrap();
    *buf_out = Some(state.mem.clone());
    *size_out = state.mem.len();
    
    state.mem.clear();
    
    true
}

pub fn zip_writer_end(zip: &mut ZipArchive) -> bool {
    // Simplified implementation
    zip.state = None;
    zip.zip_mode = ZipMode::Invalid;
    true
}

pub fn zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place(
    zip_filename: &str,
    archive_name: &str,
    buf: &[u8],
    comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    level_and_flags: u32,
) -> bool {
    zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(zip_filename, archive_name, buf, comment, level_and_flags, None)
}

pub fn zip_add_mem_to_archive_file_in_place_v2(
    zip_filename: &str,
    archive_name: &str,
    buf: &[u8],
    comment: Option<&[u8]>,
    level_and_flags: u32,
    err_out: Option<&mut ZipError>,
) -> bool {
    // Simplified implementation - in reality this would need file I/O
    let mut actual_err = ZipError::Ok;
    
    if zip_filename.is_empty() || archive_name.is_empty() || (buf.is_empty() && comment.is_some()) {
        actual_err = ZipError::InvalidParameter;
        if let Some(err) = err_out {
            *err = actual_err;
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    // Validate archive name (simplified)
    if archive_name.contains("..") || archive_name.contains(':') || archive_name.contains('\\') {
        actual_err = ZipError::InvalidFilename;
        if let Some(err) = err_out {
            *err = actual_err;
        }
        return false;
    }
    
    // Simplified: Just return true for demonstration
    // In reality, this would:
    // 1. Check if file exists
    // 2. Create new archive or append to existing
    // 3. Add the file
    // 4. Finalize
    
    if let Some(err) = err_out {
        *err = actual_err;
    }
    
    true
}

    p_archive_name: &str,
    p_comment: Option<&str>,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, usize), MzZipError> {
    // This function requires mz_zip_reader_init_file_v2, mz_zip_reader_locate_file_v2,
    // and mz_zip_reader_extract_to_heap which are not in the provided code
    // Returning an error since we can't implement without those dependencies

}

    p_zip_filename: &str,
    p_archive_name: &str,
    flags: mz_uint,
) -> Result<(Vec<u8>, usize), MzZipError> {

}

    }
}

    match p_zip {

        None => 0xFFFFFFFF, // MZ_ZIP_TYPE_INVALID
    }
}

/// Sets the last error for a zip archive and returns the previous error
pub fn mz_zip_set_last_error(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive, err_num: MzZipError) -> MzZipError {
    // This would update an error field in p_zip and return the old value
    // Since mz_zip_archive is a usize placeholder, we can't access fields
    let prev_err = MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR; // Would get from p_zip.m_last_error
    // Would set: p_zip.m_last_error = err_num;
    prev_err
}

/// Returns the last error for a zip archive without clearing it
pub fn mz_zip_peek_last_error(p_zip: Option<&mz_zip_archive>) -> MzZipError {
    match p_zip {
        Some(_) => MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR, // Would return p_zip.m_last_error
        None => MZ_ZIP_INVALID_PARAMETER,
    }
}

/// Clears the last error for a zip archive and returns the previous error
pub fn mz_zip_clear_last_error(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> MzZipError {
    mz_zip_set_last_error(p_zip, MZ_ZIP_NO_ERROR)
}

/// Returns the last error for a zip archive and clears it
pub fn mz_zip_get_last_error(p_zip: &mut mz_zip_archive) -> MzZipError {
    let prev_err = mz_zip_peek_last_error(Some(p_zip));
    let _ = mz_zip_clear_last_error(p_zip);
    prev_err
}

/// Returns a string description of an error code
pub fn mz_zip_get_error_string(mz_err: MzZipError) -> &'static str {
    match mz_err {
        0 => "no error",
        1 => "undefined error",
        2 => "too many files",
        3 => "file too large",
        4 => "unsupported method",
        5 => "unsupported encryption",
        6 => "unsupported feature",
        7 => "failed finding central directory",
        8 => "not a ZIP archive",
        9 => "invalid header or archive is corrupted",
        10 => "unsupported multidisk archive",
        11 => "decompression failed or archive is corrupted",
        12 => "compression failed",
        13 => "unexpected decompressed size",
        14 => "CRC-32 check failed",
        15 => "unsupported central directory size",
        16 => "allocation failed",
        17 => "file open failed",
        18 => "file create failed",
        19 => "file write failed",
        20 => "file read failed",
        21 => "file close failed",
        22 => "file seek failed",
        23 => "file stat failed",
        24 => "invalid parameter",
        25 => "invalid filename",
        26 => "buffer too small",
        27 => "internal error",
        28 => "file not found",
        29 => "archive is too large",
        30 => "validation failed",
        31 => "write callback failed",
        32 => "total errors",
        _ => "unknown error",
    }
}

/// Retrieves a filename from the archive
pub fn mz_zip_reader_get_filename(
    p_zip: &mz_zip_archive,
    file_index: mz_uint,
    p_filename: &mut [u8],
) -> Result<mz_uint, MzZipError> {
    // This requires mz_zip_get_cdh which is not in the provided code
    // Would get central directory header, extract filename length and copy
    let n = 0; // Would be MZ_READ_LE16(p + MZ_ZIP_CDH_FILENAME_LEN_OFS)
    
    if p_filename.len() > 0 {
        let copy_len = n.min(p_filename.len() - 1);
        // Would copy filename bytes
        p_filename[copy_len] = b'\0';
    }
    
    Ok(n + 1)
}
